the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-announces-2025-schedule-new-events-location-dates|title=UFC announces 2025 schedule including slew of challenger [[Marvin Vettori]] and [[Roman Dolidze]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/1/24/24351304/marvin-vettori-vs-roman-dolidze-2-headlines-ufc-vegas-card-in-march|title=Marvin Vettori vs. Roman Dolidze 2 headlines UFC Vegas card in March|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jed Meshew|date=2025-01-24|access-date=2025-01-24}} The pairing previously met at [[UFC 286]] in March 2023, with Vettori winning the fight via unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/03/18/ufc-286-marvin-vettori-vs-roman-dolidze/|title=UFC 286: Marvin Vettori captures decision against hard-hitting Roman Dolidze|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Dylan Rush|date=2023-03-18|access-date=2023-03-18}}\n\nA lightweight bout between Evan Elder and MarQuel Mederos was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/ufc-roundup-jalin-turner-vs-ignacio-bahamondes-added-to-ufc-313-lineup/|title=UFC roundup: Jalin Turner vs. Ignacio Bahamondes added to UFC 313 lineup|author=Sportsnet Staff|publisher=sportsnet.ca|date=2014-02-18|access-date=2025-02-18}} However, it was announced that Mederos was injured, so the bout was cancelled and Elder was moved to the [[UFC on ESPN: Hill vs. Rountree Jr.]] card.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/03/ufc-fight-night-254-marquel-mederos-injury-evan-elder-fight-off|title=UFC Fight Night 254 loses one fight due to injury|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2025-03-13|access-date=2025-03-14}} It was also reported that Mederos was moved to the [[UFC on ESPN: Moreno vs. Erceg]] two weeks later as a replacement fighter.{{Cite web|url=https://sportsnaut.com/ufc-tonight-channel-schedule/|title=UFC Tonight: How to watch UFC 313, final betting odds, and upcoming UFC proceeded at catchweight. web|url=https://www.bbc.com/sport/mixed-martial-arts/articles/cqxw35gx9p3o|title=London to host UFC Fight Night show in March 2025|publisher=bbc.com|author=BBC Sport|date=2024-11-12|access-date=2024-11-12}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Leon Edwards]] and [[Jack Della Maddalena]] was originally scheduled to headline web|title=Sean Brady steps in to face Leon Edwards in new UFC London main Heck}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Alonzo Menifield]] and Oumar Sy was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/ufc/molly-mccann-return-official-london-march-22/|title=Molly McCann's Return Announced For UFC London On March 22|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Kyle Dimond|date=2024-12-11|access-date=2024-12-11}} However, Sy withdrew from the fight due to an injury, so Menifield was moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Song]] in February against promotional newcomer Julius Walker.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-news-alonzo-menifield-nursulton-ruziboev-fights-seattle|title=UFC newcomers to battle Alonzo Menifield, Nursulton for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/ufc/molly-mccann-return-official-london-march-22/|title=Molly McCann's return announced for UFC London on March [[UFC Fight Night: Burns vs. Morales]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 316|UFC 316: Dvalishvili vs. O'Malley 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Gamrot vs. Klein''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 68''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 107''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on May 31, 2025, at the [[UFC Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/03/ufc-news-erin-blanchfield-vs-maycee-barber-main-event|title=UFC books Erin Blanchfield vs. Maycee Barber main event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-03-25|access-date=2025-03-25}}\n\n==Background==\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Erin Blanchfield]] and [[Maycee Barber]] was originally scheduled to headline the event. They were previously expected to meet at [[UFC 269]] in December 2021, but Barber withdrew due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|author=Nolan King|date=2021-11-06|title=Maycee Barber out of UFC 269 fight vs. Erin Blanchfield; replacement being sought|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/ufc-269-news-maycee-barber-out-vs-erin-blanchfield-miranda-maverick|access-date=2021-11-06|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}} At the weigh-ins, Barber weighed in at 126.5 pounds, half a pound over the women's flyweight non-title fight limit. The bout was then expected to take place at catchweight and Barber was fined 20 percent of her purse which was supposed to go to Blanchfield.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-fight-night-barber-blanchfield-multiple-changes|title=Tomorrow's UFC Fight Night hit with multiple changes after major weigh-in day drama|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2025-05-30|access-date=2025-05-30}} In turn, minutes before their walkouts were to take place, Barber had to withdraw due to a medical issue involving a seizure.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/05/31/ufc-vegas-107-main-event-cancelled-moments-before-walkouts/|title=UFC Vegas 107 Main Event Cancelled Moments Before Walkouts|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Staff|date=2025-05-31|access-date=2025-05-31}} Therefore, a lightweight bout between former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Featherweight championship|KSW Featherweight]] and [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Lightweight championship|Lightweight Champion]] [[Mateusz Gamrot]] and [[Ľudovít Klein]] inadvertently headlined the event.\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Allan Nascimento]] and Jafel Filho took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://athlonsports.com/mma/ufc-vegas-106-jafel-filho-allan-nascimento|title=UFC Vegas 106 Revives Fight That Nearly Got Away|publisher=athlonsports.com|author=Prince Horton|date=2025-02-23|access-date=2025-02-23}} The pairing was originally scheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Tybura vs. Spivac 2]] in August 2024 but was cancelled as a result of Nascimento being ill.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/08/06/ufc-vegas-95-hit-with-last-minute-cancelations/|title=UFC Vegas 95 hit with last minute cancelations|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Staff|date=2024-08-06|access-date=2024-08-06}} At the weigh-ins, Nascimento weighed in at 127.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and Nascimento was fined 20 percent of his purse which went to Filho.\n\nA women's strawweight bout between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Rayanne dos Santos]] and Alice Ardelean took place for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://oimpacto.com.br/2025/04/04/em-busca-da-primeira-vitoria-no-ufc-paraense-rayanne-dos-santos-encara-romena-em-maio/|title=In search of her first UFC victory, Rayanne dos Santos from Pará faces Romanian in May|publisher=oimpacto.com.br|author=Staff|date=2025-04-04|access-date=2025-04-07}} {{in lang|pt-br}} They were originally scheduled to compete at [[UFC Fight Night: Hernandez vs. Pereira]] in October 2024, but dos Santos withdrew from the bout due to a broken arm.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/09/ufc-fight-night-244-rayanne-dos-santos-broken-arm-training-out-alice-ardelean|title=Rayanne Dos Santos out of UFC Fight Night 244 due to broken arm|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2024-09-06|access-date=2024-09-07}}\n\nA women's featherweight bout between [[Ketlen Vieira]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters]] featherweight winner [[Macy Chiasson]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/4/2/24399690/ketlen-vieira-vs-macy-chiasson-rebooked-for-ufc-vegas-107|title=Ketlen Vieira vs. Macy Chiasson rebooked for UFC Vegas 107|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2025-04-02|access-date=2025-04-02}} The bout was originally scheduled to be a bantamweight bout but on the day of the weigh-ins, it was announced that it would be changed to featherweight due to \"weight management issues\" from Vieira, who in turn forfeited 25% of her purse. The pair was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Ankalaev vs. Walker 2]] in January 2024, but Vieira was pulled as a result of an injury and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2023-12-30|title=Loss of Macy Chiasson vs. Ketlen Vieira among hits to UFC Vegas 84|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/12/30/loss-macy-chiasson-ketlen-vieira-ufc-vegas-84/|access-date=2024-06-17|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} They were later expected to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Song]] in February, but due to an injury suffered by Chiasson, the bout was removed from the card.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Seattle-Lineup-Shifts-Due-to-Injury-New-Added-Matches-196154|title=UPDATED UFC Seattle lineup shifts due to injury, new added matches|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2024-12-13|access-date=2025-02-13}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Jeremiah Wells]] and promotional newcomer Andreas Gustafsson was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|title=ONGOING: Andreas Gustafsson's new opponent in May!|url=https://www.mmanytt.se/senaste-nytt/pa-gang-andreas-gustafssons-nya-motstandare-i-maj-j6/|date=2025-04-11|access-date=2025-04-11|publisher=mmanytt.se|author=Jack Elmer}} {{in lang|sv}} However on May 20, Wells withdrew due to an injury and was replaced by [[Trevin Giles]] in a 180 pound catchweight bout.{{Cite web|title=Andreas Gustafsson loses his opponent before his UFC during the weigh-ins, the UFC announced that the pairing was canceled due to Giles suffering a head injury after falling in his bathroom, so Gustafsson was booked for the following week at [[UFC 316]] with a new opponent.{{Cite web|title=Andreas Gustafsson's UFC debut is canceled - new match in the Figueiredo]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Burns vs. Morales]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 315: Muhammad vs. Della Maddalena''''' was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on May 10, 2025, at the [[Bell Centre]] in [[Montreal]], [[Quebec]], Canada.{{cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/montreals-bell-centre-set-to-host-ufc-315-in-may/|title=Montreal's Bell Centre set to host UFC 315 in May|publisher=sportsnet.ca|author=Staff|date=2025-02-07|access-date=2025-02-07}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's eighth visit to Montreal and first since [[UFC 186]] in April 2015.\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Belal Muhammad]] and [[Jack Della Maddalena]] headlined this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-315-fight-card-montreal-muhammad-della-maddalenna-shevchenko-fiorot|title=UFC 315 title fights revealed: Belal Muhammad, Valentina Shevchenko set to defend in Montreal|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-02-19|access-date=2025-02-19}} [[Ian Machado Garry]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/04/27/ian-machado-garry-official-back-up-for-ufc-315-title-fight/83308716007/|title=Dana White: Ian Machado Garry official back-up for UFC 315 welterweight title fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2025-04-27|access-date=2025-04-28}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] bout between current two-time champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] and [[Manon Fiorot]] took place in the co-main event.\n\nA welterweight bout between former welterweight title challenger [[Gilbert Burns]] and [[Michael Morales (fighter)|Michael Morales]] was scheduled for [[UFC 314]].{{cite web|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/gilbert-burns-fights-against-ko-star-ufc-314/|title=Report – Gilbert Burns books return fight against unbeaten KO star at UFC 314 in Miami|publisher=lowkickmma.com|author=Ross Markey|date=2025-01-09|access-date=2025-01-10}} However, the pairing was moved to this event due to undisclosed reasons. In turn, the bout was moved to [[UFC Fight Night: Burns vs. Morales]] to serve as the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://athlonsports.com/mma/ufc-vegas-106-gilbert-burns-vs-michael-morales|title=UFC Vegas 106 adds a high-stakes test for undefeated prospect – top 10 spot in reach|publisher=athlonsports.net|author=Prince Horton|date=2025-04-11|access-date=2025-04-11}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Nora Cornolle]] and Hailey Cowan was expected to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-315-hailey-cowan-vs-nora-cornolle-montreal|title=UFC 315 targets Hailey Cowan vs. Nora Cornolle addition to Montreal lineup|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun|date=2025-02-16|access-date=2025-02-16}} However, the bout was moved to [[UFC 314]] for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/features/matchmaking-bulletin/ufc-new-fights-2025/|title=UFC matchmaking bulletin: new fights for Gaethje, Mitchell, Hill, Aldo, and more|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2025-03-02|access-date=2025-03-03}}\n\n[[Gavin Tucker]] was expected to face Lee Jeong-yeong in a featherweight bout at the preliminary card.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/04/16/gavin-tucker-joins-ufc-315-faces-jeong-yeong-lee/|title=Gavin Tucker joins UFC 315, faces Jeong Yeong (fighter)|Daniel Santos]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-315-tucker-lee-cancellation|title=UFC 315 reportedly loses promising prospect fight|publisher=si.com|author=Mathew Riddle|date=2025-04-29|access-date=2025-04-29}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-315-report-daniel-santos-jeong-yeong-lee|title=UFC 315 reportedly gets last-minute web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/benoit-saint-denis-joel-alvarez-added-ufc-315|title=UFC reportedly adds savage finisher matchup to Montreal PPV|publisher=si.com|author=Mathew Riddle|date=2025-03-31|access-date=2025-03-31}} However, Álvarez withdrew from the fight due to a hand injury and was replaced by returning veteran [[Kyle Prepolec]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/joel-alvarez-out-of-ufc-315-bout-with-benoit-saint-denis-with-hand-injury/|title=Joel Alvarez out of UFC 315 bout vs. Benoit Saint-Denis with hand card|publisher=sportsnet.ca|author=Staff|date=2025-05-05|access-date=2025-05-05}}\n\nOriginally scheduled as a bantamweight bout, the fight between former two-time [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] and [[UFC Hall of Fame]]r (also former [[List of WEC champions#Featherweight Championship|WEC Featherweight Champion]]) [[José Aldo]] and [[Aiemann Zahabi]] was moved to featherweight on the day of the weigh-ins, with Aldo weighing in at 143 pounds and Zahabi at 142 pounds. After the official weigh-ins, Zahabi disclosed that his team was told Aldo's weight cut was going to be a problem and said he was just under 137 pounds at the time. Had he made weight, Aldo's weight discrepancy would have meant an automatic cancellation of the bout. Instead, Zahabi stopped his weight cut and had to rehydrate for a fight at featherweight so he could be close enough to Aldo's missed weight to avoid the fight being scrapped entirely.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/05/09/ufc-315-jose-aldo-weight-cut-issues-vs-aiemann-zahabi-featherweight-bantamweight-weigh-ins/83532420007/|title=UFC 315: Jose Aldo, Aiemann Zahabi bantamweight bout moved to featherweight at weigh-ins|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2025-05-05|access-date=2025-05-10}}\n\nIn addition, at the weigh-ins, [[Bruno Silva (fighter)|Bruno Silva]] weighed in at 187 pounds, a pound over the weight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and Silva was fined 20 percent of his purse which went to his opponent [[Marc-André Barriault]].{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/article/ufc-315-weigh-results-jose-221816331.html|title=UFC 315 weigh-in results: Jose Aldo struggles lead to change in contracted weight|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2025-05-10|access-date=2025-05-10}}\n\nDuring the event's broadcast, former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] and [[List of UFC Champions#Tournament Winners|UFC 12 Heavyweight Tournament winner]] [[Vitor Belfort]] was announced as the next \"pioneer wing\" [[UFC Hall of Fame]] inductee during International Fight Week festivities in Las Vegas this June.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/05/10/vitor-belfort-2025-ufc-hall-of-fame-class/83545968007/|title=Former champ Vitor Belfort joins 2025 UFC Hall of Fame Fighting Championship]] that took place on June 28, 2025, at [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-317-international-fight-week-las-vegas-june|title=UFC 317 headlines International Fight Week 2025 in late June|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King |date=2025-02-11|access-date=2025-02-11}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was held during the UFC's annual International Fight Week. The 2025 [[UFC Hall of Fame]] induction ceremony also took place during the week.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/2/11/24363543/ufc-reveals-dates-for-ufc-317-international-fight-week-2025 |title=UFC reveals dates for UFC 317, International Fight Week 2025|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-02-11|access-date=2025-02-11}}\n\nA [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former [[UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Ilia Topuria]] and former champion [[Charles Oliveira]] served as the event's headliner. Current champion [[Islam Makhachev]] vacated the title in order to challenge for the [[UFC Welterweight Championship]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2025/5/13/24429594/dana-white-drops-ufc-317-fight-ilia-topuria-vs-charles-oliveira-headlines-vacant-lightweight-title|title=Vegas heat! Dana White drops UFC 317 fight card - Ilia Topuria vs. Charles Oliveira headlines for vacant lightweight title|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2025-05-13|access-date=2025-05-13}} Topuria vacated his featherweight title in April in order to move up to lightweight.{{Cite web|title=UFC 314 headlined by Alexander Volkanovski vs. Diego Lopes title bout with Ilia Topuria vacating|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-314-fight-card-volkanovski-vs-lopes|date=2025-02-19|access-date=2025-02-19|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} [[Arman Tsarukyan]] (who was previously scheduled to challenge for the title at [[UFC 311]] but pulled out a day before the event due to injury) served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/6/16/24450393/arman-tsarukyan-backing-up-ufc-317-main-event-between-ilia-topuria-and-charles-oliveira|title=Arman Tsarukyan backing up UFC 317 main event between Ilia Topuria and Charles Oliveira|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-06-16|access-date=2025-06-17}}\n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Alexandre Pantoja]] and former interim title challenger [[Kai Kara-France]] co-headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.telecomasia.net/news/mma/source-alexandre-pantoja-to-face-kai-kara-france-on-june-29-at-ufc-317/|title=Source: Alexandre Pantoja to Face Kai Kara-France on June 29 at UFC 317|publisher=telecomasia.net|author=Marina Magomedova|date=2025-04-06|access-date=2025-04-09}} The bout was originally rumored to take place at [[UFC 314]] and [[UFC 316]], but the bookings were never confirmed.{{Cite web|date=2025-01-29|title=Report: Alexandre Pantoja vs. Kai Kara-France set for UFC 314 in Miami on April of Champions]] in November 2016, when Pantoja won the two-round bout by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2016/11/episode-no-8-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-24-tournament-of-champions|title=Episode No. 8 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 24: Tournament of Champions'|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2016-11-02|access-date=2025-02-15}}\n\nA flyweight bout between former title challenger (also former [[Legacy_Fighting_Alliance#LFA_Flyweight_Championship|LFA Flyweight Champion]]) [[Brandon Royval]] and former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|RIZIN Bantamweight Champion]] [[Manel Kape]] was scheduled to take part at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/04/ufc-317-manel-kape-vs-brandon-royval-rebooked|title=Manel Kape vs. Brandon Royval booked for UFC 317|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura and Mike Bohn|date=2025-04-03|access-date=2025-04-03}} They were originally scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Kape vs. Almabayev]] in a title eliminator, but Royval had to withdraw due to multiple concussions.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-news-brandon-royval-vs-manel-kape-canceled|title=UFC bantamweight bout between former interim [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Cory Sandhagen]] and former two-time [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-des-moines-deiveson-figueiredo-vs-cory-sandhagen-main-event-booked|title=Deiveson Figueiredo vs. Cory Texas]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/05/31/noche-ufc-moved-from-guadalajara-to-san-antonio-texas/|title=Noche UFC moved from Guadalajara to San Antonio, ESPN: Vera vs. Sandhagen]] in March 2023.\n\nIt was the third annual \"Noche UFC\" event commemorating [[Independence Day (Mexico)|Mexico's independence day]], following September 2023's [[UFC Fight Night: Grasso vs. Shevchenko 2]] and September 2024's [[UFC 306]]. The card was originally expected to take place in [[Guadalajara]], Mexico and serve as UFC 320, but it was moved to San Antonio (and shifted to a Fight Night event) due to the construction of Arena Guadalajara being stalled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/03/ufc-320-noche-guadalajara-mexico-announcement|title=Noche UFC heads to Mexico for first time as UFC 320 set for (fighter)|Diego Lopes]] and [[Jean Silva]] headlined the event.{{Cite event.{{Cite web|title=Rob Font kontra Raul Rosas Jr. na wrześniowej gali Noche Ange Loosa: $30,000\n*'''Jamey-Lyn Horth''': $50,000 (includes $25,000 win bonus) def. Vanessa Demopoulos: $65,000\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn July 23, 2025, in response to an appeal filed by [[Cody Brundage]], the Georgia Athletic and Entertainment Commission (GAEC) overturned the initial technical decision loss originally attributed to Brundage following a headbutt that rendered him unable to continue. The bout against Mansur Abdul-Malik was subsequently declared a majority draw, in accordance with GAEC regulations which prohibit scoring incomplete rounds. The judges' scores for the two completed rounds were 19–19, 19–19, and 18–20.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/7/23/24473226/cody-brundage-loss-to-mansur-abdul-malik-overturned-by-georgia-commission|title=Cody Brundage loss to Mansur Abdul-Malik overturned by Georgia commission|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-07-23|access-date=2025-07-23}}\n\n== See also ==\n*[[2025 in UFC]]\n*[[List of current UFC fighters]]\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n\n== [[Jamahal Hill]] and former title challenger [[Khalil Rountree Jr.]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/04/ufc-baku-jamahal-hill-vs-khalil-rountree-headlines-debut-azerbaijan|title=Jamahal Hill vs. Khalil Rountree headlines UFC's debut in Azerbaijan|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-04-10|access-date=2025-04-11}} The pairing was originally expected to meet at [[UFC 303]] in June 2024, but the bout was scrapped as Rountree withdrew from the event after unintentionally ingesting [[DHEA]] in a tainted supplement.{{Cite web|author=Staff|date=2024-05-19|title=Khalil Rountree out of UFC 303 due to banned substance|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/05/18/khalil-rountree-out-of-ufc-303-due-to-banned-substance/|access-date=2024-05-19|publisher=cagesidepress.com}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2024/05/ufc-khalil-rountree-failed-drug-test-supplement-company-mistake-suspension|title=UFC: Khalil Rountree failed drug test due to supplement company's mistake, suspension reduced to 2 months|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2024-05-22|access-date=2024-05-22}} They were then booked to headline [[UFC on ESPN: Machado Garry vs. Prates]] in April, but Hill pulled out due to a lingering leg injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/2/26/24373863/jamahal-hill-vs-khalil-rountree-jr-set-to-headline-ufc-kansas-city-in-april|title=Jamahal Hill vs. Khalil Rountree Jr. set to headline UFC Kansas City in April|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-02-26|access-date=2025-02-26}}{{Cite web|url=https://tatame.com.br/jamahal-hill-deixa-luta-rountree-jornalista-saiba/|title=Out of action? Jamahal Hill drops out of fight with Khalil Rountree, according to journalist; find out more|publisher=tatame.com.br|author=Mateus Machado|date=2025-04-01|access-date=2025-04-01}} {{in lang|pt-br}} \n\nThe co-main event was originally set to feature a flyweight bout between [[Tagir Ulanbekov]] and former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Kyoji Horiguchi]] (who is also a former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Flyweight Championship|RIZIN Flyweight]] and two-time [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|RIZIN Bantamweight Champion]], as well as former [[List of Bellator champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bellator Bantamweight World Champion]]). Horiguchi would make his promotional return for the first time since November 2016.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2025/4/11/24406504/rizin-champion-kyoji-horiguchi-ufc-return-opposite-team-khabib-tagir-ulanbekov-ufc-baku|title=RIZIN champion Kyoji Horiguchi books UFC return opposite Team Khabib’s Tagir Ulanbekov at UFC Baku|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Andrew Richardson|date=2017-04-11|access-date=2025-04-11}} In turn, Horiguchi pulled out for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by Azat Maksum.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/05/19/ufc-baku-kyoji-horiguchis-return-off-tagir-ulanbekov-azat-maksum/83731397007/|title=Kyoji Horiguchi's UFC return delayed, Tagir Ulanbekov gets new opponent in Baku|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-05-19|access-date=2025-05-19}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between former interim [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Curtis Blaydes]] and promotional newcomer [[Rizvan Kuniev]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2025/3/26/24394328/fifth-times-a-charm-curtis-blaydes-vs-rizvan-kuniev-moved-again-to-azerbaijan|title=Fifth time's a charm? Curtis Blaydes vs. Rizvan Kuniev moved (again) to Azerbaijan|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2025-03-26|access-date=2025-03-26}} The pairing was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Song]],{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/12/13/24320604/curtis-blaydes-return-set-for-ufc-seattle|title=Curtis Blaydes' return set for UFC Seattle|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2024-12-13|access-date=2025-02-12}} then shifted to [[UFC 313]] and eventually cancelled a few hours before the event after Blaydes had to pull out due to an undisclosed illness.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-313-card-curtis-blaydes-vs-rizvan-kuniev-shifted-from-seattle|title=Curtis Blaydes vs. Rizvan Kuniev shifted from Seattle to UFC 313 in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-02-12|access-date=2025-02-12}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/ufc/ufc-313-fights-canceled-including-blaydes/|title=Two UFC 313 fights canceled hours before the event, including Curtis Blaydes' return|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2025-03-08|access-date=2025-03-08}} The bout was later rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Burns vs. Morales]] but was subsequently moved to this event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2025/3/26/24394328/fifth-times-a-charm-curtis-blaydes-vs-rizvan-kuniev-moved-again-to-azerbaijan|title=Fifth time's a charm? Curtis Blaydes vs. Rizvan Kuniev moved (again) to Azerbaijan|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2025-03-26|access-date=2025-03-26}}\n\nAnother couple of fights were also scheduled for UFC Fight Night: Burns vs. Morales and moved to this event due to undisclosed reasons: a middleweight bout between [[Jun Yong Park|Park Jun-yong]] and [[Ismail Naurdiev]]{{Cite web|url=https://www.mk.co.kr/en/sports/11250334|title=Park Jun-yong will face Ismail Nourdiev (29, Austria/Morocco) in a middleweight (84kg) match at the UFC Fight Night Qatar on May 17.|publisher=mk.co.kr|author=Kang Daeho|date=2025-02-26|access-date=2025-02-26}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/features/matchmaking-bulletin/ufc-latest-fights/|title=Latest UFC Fight Announcements Featuring Rose Namajunas, Paul Craig, And More|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2025-03-23|access-date=2025-03-23}} and a heavyweight bout between [[Hamdy Abdelwahab]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Peña vs. Team Nunes]] heavyweight winner Mohammed Usman.{{Cite web|url=https://www.yardbarker.com/mma/articles/brother_of_former_ufc_champion_fighting_to_save_his_career/s1_17615_41822744|title=Brother of Former UFC Champion Fighting to Save His Career|publisher=yardbarker.com|author=Michael Aldon|date=2025-02-26|access-date=2025-02-26}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/features/matchmaking-bulletin/ufc-new-title-fights/|title=UFC Matchmaking Bulletin: New Fights For O'Malley, Garry, Kape, And More|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2025-04-06|access-date=2025-04-06}}\n\nOriginally slated as a lightweight bout, the fight between newcomer [[Tofiq Musayev]] and [[Myktybek Orolbai]] proceeded at a catchweight of 165 pounds. Initially there was no indication of why and when the change was made, but both competitors exceeded the lightweight non-title fight limit during the weigh-ins, with Musayev weighing 163 pounds and Orolbai 165 pounds.{{Cite web|date=2025-06-20|title=Highly anticipated UFC Baku debut receives surprising late notice change during weigh-ins|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2025/06/20/highly-anticipated-ufc-baku-debut-receives-surprising-late-notice-change-during-weigh-ins/|access-date=2025-06-21|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Kyle Dimond}} It was revealed after the event that the change was related to Orolbai not being able to cut all the weight during fight week, therefore moving up the weight limit for the bout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/06/21/ufc-baku-bonuses-nazim-sadykhov-nikolas-motta-100000-each/84303449007/|title=UFC on ABC 8 bonuses: Nazim Sadykhov’s wild war with [[UFC 320|UFC 320: Ankalaev vs. Pereira 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Ulberg vs. Reyes''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 260''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 118''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on 28 September 2025, at the [[RAC Arena]] in [[Perth]], Australia.{{Cite web|author=Nolan King|title=UFC announces return to Perth|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/07/14/ufc-news-perth-event-2025/85200417007/|date=2025-07-15|access-date=2025-07-15|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event marked the promotion's fourth visit to Perth and its first-ever Fight Night, following [[UFC 305]] in August 2024.\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Carlos Ulberg]] and former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Dominick Reyes]] headlined this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/7/29/24477017/carlos-ulberg-vs-dominick-reyes-headlines-ufcs-return-to-perth|title=Carlos Ulberg vs. Dominick Reyes headlines UFC's return to Perth|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-07-29|access-date=2025-07-29}} The pairing was initially scheduled for [[UFC 297]] in January 2024, but was cancelled due to an injury sustained by Ulberg.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/dominick-reyes-ufc-return-opponent-revealed|title=Dominick Reyes' Return Booked For UFC 297, Opponent Revealed|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Staff|date=2023-11-12|access-date=2023-11-12}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/12/ufc-297-news-dominick-reyes-says-carlos-ulberg-out-injury-toronto-replacement|title='Frustrated' Dominick Reyes says Carlos Ulberg out of UFC 297 due to injury |publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2023-12-30|access-date=2023-12-30}} It was later rebooked for [[UFC on ESPN: Blanchfield vs. Fiorot]] in March 2024; however, Reyes withdrew from the bout due to [[deep vein thrombosis]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2024/1/23/24048536/dominick-reyes-out-alonzo-menifield-now-faces-carlos-ulberg-at-ufc-atlantic-city|title=Dominick Reyes out, Alonzo Menifield now faces Carlos Ulberg at UFC Atlantic City|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2024-01-23|access-date=2024-01-23}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Choi Doo-ho]] and [[Daniel Santos (fighter)|Daniel Santos]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=UFC negocia Daniel Willycat vs Doo Ho Choi para card na Austrália, em setembro|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/ufc-negocia-daniel-willycat-vs-doo-ho-choi-para-card-na-australia-em-setembro/|date=2025-07-08|access-date=2025-07-13|publisher=agfight.com|author=Geovanne web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/ufc-perth-adds-three-more-australian-fighters-to-fight-night-card/|title=UFC Perth adds three more Australian fighters to Fight Night following fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-bonuses-and-awards/387481/ufc-paris-results-post-fight-bonus-winners-espn-record-knockouts-saint-denis-jones-delija-fernandes|title=Bonuses! UFC Paris card sets record with nine total knockout finishes|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2025-09-06|access-date=2025-09-06}}\n*'''Fight of the Night:''' No bonus awarded.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Benoît Saint Denis''', '''Mason Jones''', '''Ante Delija''', and '''Kauê Fernandes'''\n\n==Records set==\nThe event set a new UFC record for the [[List_of_UFC_records#Most_knockouts_-_In_a_single_event|most knockouts in a single event]], recording nine knockouts and surpassing the previous record of eight. [[UFC 332]] later surpassed it with eleven knockouts in October 2026.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/50095968/ufc-332-sees-record-9-first-round-knockouts-11-total-finishes|title=UFC 332 sees record 9 first-round knockouts, 11 total finishes|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2026-10-04|access-date=2026-10-04}}\n\nThe event also matched the UFC record for the [[List_of_UFC_records#Most_Finishes_-_In_a_single_event|most finishes in a single event]], tying [[UFC Fight Night: Rockhold vs. Bisping]], [[UFC 224]], [[UFC 281]], [[UFC 329]] and [[UFC 332]] with a total of 11 finishes.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2025/09/07/ufc-paris-tied-three-other-cards-for-a-record-after-legend-said-it-might-be-the-greatest-ufc-event-of-all-time/|title=UFC Paris tied three other cards for a record after legend said it might be 'the greatest UFC event of all time'|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Kyle Dimond|date=2025-09-07|access-date=2025-09-07}} The record was ultimately surpassed in August 2026 when [[UFC Fight Night: Medić vs. Rodriguez]] recorded twelve finishes.{{Cite web|url=https://heavy.com/sports/ufc/ufc-belgrade-bonuses-revealed-after-card/|title=UFC Belgrade bonuses revealed after record-setting card|publisher=heavy.com|author=Adam at the [[Smoothie King Center]] in [[New Orleans, Louisiana]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/04/25/max-holloway-vs-dustin-poirier-3-announced-for-ufc-318-in-louisiana/|title=Max Holloway vs. Dustin Poirier 3 announced for UFC 318 in Louisiana|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2025-04-25|access-date=2025-04-25}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's sixth visit to New Orleans and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Boetsch vs. Henderson]] in June 2015.\n\nFormer [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Max Holloway]] put his symbolic \"BMF\" (''baddest [[motherfucker]]'') title on the line against former interim [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Dustin Poirier]] in a lightweight bout in the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/04/25/ufc-318-dustin-poirier-retirement-fight-max-holloway-bmf-title-new-orleans/83232284007/|title=Dustin Poirier's retirement fight set for UFC 318 vs. Max Holloway for BMF title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-04-25|access-date=2025-04-25}} This was their third meeting and Poirier's retirement fight. The pairing first met at [[UFC 143]] in February 2012, when Holloway made his promotional debut and lost by a triangle-armbar submission.{{cite web|author=Steve Cofield|title=Dustin Poirier schools young Max Holloway with wicked submission at UFC 143|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/blogs/mma-cagewriter/poirier-schools-young-holloway-wicked-submission-ufc-143-024356569.html|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|date=2012-02-05|access-date=2012-02-05}} Their second outing was in April 2019 at [[UFC 236]] when Poirier became the interim lightweight champion after winning via unanimous decision, which also ended Holloway's 13-fight win streak.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2019/04/14/ufc-236-results-dustin-poirier-outlasts-max-holloway-in-stunning-battle-to-claim-interim-belt/82050381007/|title=UFC 236 results: Dustin Poirier outlasts Max Holloway in stunning battle to claim interim belt|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=John Morgan|date=2025-04-19|access-date=2025-04-25}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Marvin Vettori]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Middleweight Championship|LFA Middleweight Champion]] [[Brendan Allen]] took place in the preliminary card of the event.{{Cite web|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2025-05-07|title=Bad Blood! Brendan Allen vs. Marvin Vettori (re)booked for UFC 318 in New Orleans|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2025/5/7/24425877/bad-blood-brendan-allen-vs-marvin-vettori-re-booked-for-ufc-318-in-new-orleans|access-date=2025-05-07|publisher=mmamania.com}} The pairing was previously scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Allen vs. Curtis 2]] in April 2024, but Vettori withdrew due to an injury.{{Cite web|date=2024-03-14|title=Marvin Vettori withdraws from UFC Fight Night main event vs. Brendan Allen|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2024/03/14/ufc-news-marvin-vettori-out-vs-brendan-allen/79973471007/|access-date=2025-05-07|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farah Hannoun}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Islam Dulatov]] and Adam Fugitt took place at the event.{{Cite web|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2025-05-07|title=Fight news: Contender Series standout Islam Dulatov rebooked against Adam Fugitt for UFC 318|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2025/5/7/24425775/fight-news-contender-series-standout-islam-dulatov-rebooked-against-adam-fugitt-for-ufc-317|access-date=2025-05-07|publisher=mmamania.com}} The pairing was previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC Fight Night: Cejudo vs. Song]] in last February, but Dulatov was forced to withdraw due to a serious injury.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|date=2025-01-28|title=Islam Dulatov out of UFC debut on Feb. 22, Billy Goff and [[Gunnar Nelson (fighter)|Gunnar Nelson]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/3286789/magny-nelson-added-to-ufc-318|title=Magny-Nelson added to UFC 318|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2025-05-16|access-date=2025-05-16}} They were previously scheduled to meet in May 2018 at [[UFC Fight Night: Thompson vs. Till]] but Nelson was forced to withdraw due to a knee injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmaweekly.com/news/gunnar-nelson-out-of-co-main-event-fight-against-neil-magny-at-ufc-in-liverpool|title=Gunnar Nelson Out of Co-Main [[UFC 317|UFC 317: Topuria vs. Oliveira]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 318|UFC 318: Holloway vs. Poirier 3]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Lewis vs. Teixeira''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 70''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on July 12, 2025, at [[Bridgestone Arena]] in [[Nashville, Tennessee]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.tennessean.com/story/sports/2025/05/15/ufc-fight-night-nashville-july-dana-white-derrick-lewis/83649363007/|title=UFC Fight Night coming to Nashville in July|publisher=tennessean.com|author=Harrison Campbell|date=2025-05-15|access-date=2025-05-19}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's seventh visit to Nashville and first since [[UFC on ESPN: Sandhagen vs. Font]] in August 2023.\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] and undefeated prospect [[Tallison Teixeira]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/derrick-lewis-fight-tallison-teixeira-ufc-nashville|title=Derrick Lewis to fight 6'8\" wrecking machine in UFC main event|publisher=si.com|author=Mathew Riddle|date=2025-04-02|access-date=2025-04-02}}\n\n[[Junior Tafa]] faced Tuco Tokkos in a light heavyweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/5/27/24438270/ufc-nashville-adds-several-new-fights-to-card-headlined-by-derrick-lewis-vs-tallison-teixeira|title=UFC Nashville adds several new fights to card headlined by Derrick Lewis vs. Tallison Teixeira|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jed Meshew|date=2025-05-27|access-date=2025-05-27}} They were originally scheduled to compete at [[UFC on ESPN: Sandhagen vs. Figueiredo]] in May, but both Tafa and Tokkos were forced to withdraw due to an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://athlonsports.com/mma/ufc-fighter-release-tuco-tokkos-junior-tafa|title=UFC fighter facing release takes on much bigger Opponent|publisher=athlonsports.com|author=Michael Aldon|date=2025-02-26|access-date=2025-02-26}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-des-moines-loses-junior-tafa|title=UFC Des Moines reportedly loses heavyweight banger fight|publisher=si.com|author=Matthew Riddle|date=2025-04-24|access-date=2025-04-24}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/04/29/ufc-des-moines-junior-tafa-vs-tuco-tokkos-canceled-injuries/83345541007/|title=UFC Des Moines loses fight due to double injuries|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-04-29|access-date=2025-04-29}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Ateba Gautier]] and Robert Valentin was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-ateba-gautier-robert-valentin-nashville|title='Mini Francis Ngannou' gets second UFC fight booked after knockout debut|publisher=si.com|author=Mathew Riddle|date=2025-05-24|access-date=2025-05-24}} However, the bout was moved to [[UFC 318]] one week later for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-318-dustin-poirier-adds-pitbull-ige|title=Dustin Poirier's retirement card reportedly adds banger fight|publisher=si.com|author=Mathew Riddle|date=2025-06-04|access-date=2025-06-04}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN / ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Derrick Lewis]] \n|def.\n|[[Tallison $11,014,682\n|previousevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Dolidze vs. Hernandez]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Walker vs. Zhang]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 319: du Plessis vs. Chimaev''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 16, 2025, at the [[United Center]] in [[Chicago, Illinois]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/5/13/24429684/dricus-du-plessis-defends-middleweight-title-against-khamzat-chimaev-at-ufc-319-in-chicago|title=Dricus du Plessis defends middleweight title against Khamzat Chimaev at UFC 319 in Chicago|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Jed Meshew|date=2025-05-13|access-date=2025-05-13}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's eighth visit to Chicago and first since [[UFC 238]] in June 2019.\n\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] bout between current champion (also former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Welterweight championship|KSW Welterweight Champion]]) [[Dricus du Plessis]] and undefeated contender [[Khamzat Chimaev]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-319-fight-card-dricus-du-plessis-to-defend-title-against-khamzat-chimaev-in-chicago-in-august/|title=UFC 319 fight card: Dricus du Plessis to defend title against Khamzat Chimaev in Chicago in August|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Shakiel Mahjouri|date=2025-05-13|access-date=2025-05-13}} [[Nassourdine Imavov]] was expected to serve as backup and potential replacement for this fight, but he announced a month later that it would not be the case anymore as he was angling for a fight against [[Caio Borralho]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/05/14/ufc-319-dricus-du-plessis-vs-khamzat-chimaev-official-backup-imavov/83621199007/|title=Nassourdine Imavov named official backup for Du Plessis vs. Chimaev at UFC 319|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2025-05-14|access-date=2025-05-14}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/06/17/ufc-319-nassourdine-imavov-no-longer-backup-shouts-top-10-opponent/84251830007/|title=Nassourdine Imavov abandons UFC 319 backup role, turns attention to Caio Borralho|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2025-06-17|access-date=2025-06-18}} In turn, Borralho announced that he would be the new backup for the main event. He and Imavov are still scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Imavov vs. Borralho]] three weeks later.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/7/28/24476034/caio-borralho-says-dricus-du-plessis-vs-khamzat-chimaev-main-event-at-ufc-319|title=Caio Borralho says he's the backup fighter for Dricus Du Plessis vs. Khamzat Chimaev main event at UFC 319|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2025-07-28|access-date=2025-07-28}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Geoff Neal]] and [[Carlos Prates]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/07/13/ufc-319-chicago-fight-card-geoff-neal-vs-carlos-prates/84437777007/ |title= UFC 319 adds Geoff Neal vs. Carlos Prates to Chicago|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-07-13|access-date=2025-07-13}} They were originally scheduled to meet at [[UFC 314]] in April, but Neal had to withdraw from the fight due to an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-314-main-card-cancellation|title=UFC 314 hit by huge main card cancellation|publisher=si.com|author=Mathew Riddle|date=2025-03-29|access-date=2025-03-29}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Karine Silva]] and [[JJ Aldrich]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=Card numerado! Ultimate encaminha Karine Killer vs JJ Aldrich para o UFC 319, em agosto|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/card-numerado-ultimate-encaminha-karine-killer-vs-jj-aldrich-para-o-ufc-319-em-agosto/|date=2025-05-28|access-date=2025-05-28|publisher=agfight.com|author=Gaspar Bruno}} {{in lang|pt-br}} However, Aldrich withdrew from the fight for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by Dione Barbosa.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/7/30/24477479/karine-silva-rematch-dione-barbosa-jj-aldrich-ufc-319|title=Karine Silva gets new opponent after JJ Aldrich withdraws from UFC 319 fight|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-07-30|access-date=2025-07-31}}\n\nThe flyweight final of [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Cormier vs. Team Sonnen]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/the-ultimate-fighter-33-finale-bouts-added-to-ufc-319-preliminary-card|title=The Ultimate Fighter 33 finale bouts added to UFC 319 preliminary limit. was scheduled to headline this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/3285937|title=Albazi, Taira to meet in Aug. 2 UFC main event|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick was originally scheduled to compete a week later against former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Steve Erceg]].{{cite a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 23, 2025, at the [[Shanghai Indoor Stadium]] in [[Shanghai]], China.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/05/22/ufc-news-fight-night-set-shanghai-summer/83785595007/|title=UFC returns to Shanghai in August for Fight Night event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2025-05-22|access-date=2025-05-22}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's second visit to Shanghai and first since [[UFC Fight Night: Bisping vs. Gastelum]] in November 2017. It was also the first time since the [[COVID-19 pandemic]] that the UFC has returned to Mainland China. The promotion was originally expected to make their return to the location at [[UFC Fight Night: Song vs. Gutiérrez]] in December 2023, before moving that event to Las Vegas due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/11/15/23962824/ufc-shanghai-card-expected-to-shift-to-las-vegas-on-dec-9|title=UFC Shanghai card expected to shift to Las Vegas on Dec. 9|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2023-11-15|access-date=2025-05-22}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Johnny Walker (fighter)|Johnny Walker]] and [[Zhang Mingyang]] headlined this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.telecomasia.net/news/mma/source-walker-to-fight-zhang-on-august-23-at-ufc-shanghai-event/|title=Source: Walker to fight Zhang on August 23 at UFC Shanghai event|publisher=telecomasia.net|author=Marina Magomedova|date=2025-05-21|access-date=2025-05-22}}\n\nA catchweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Brian Ortega]] and former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Aljamain Sterling]] took place in a five round co-headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/06/25/ufc-shanghai-gets-headliner-plus-brian-ortega-vs-aljamain-sterling/84363498007/|title=Brian Ortega vs. Aljamain Sterling added to UFC Shanghai; Walker vs. Zhang headlines|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-06-25|access-date=2025-06-25}} Originally slated as a featherweight bout, it was changed to a catchweight of 153 pounds after reports that Ortega had complications during his weight cut.{{cite web|author=Damon Martin|title=UFC Shanghai weigh-in results: Brian Ortega vs. Aljamain Sterling changed to catchweight fight |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/400350/ufc-shanghai-weigh-in-results-brian-ortega-vs-aljamain-sterling-changed-to-catchweight-fight|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2025-08-21|date=2025-08-21}}\n\nShi Ming and Bruna Brasil were expected to meet in a women's strawweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/ambiente-hostil-bruna-brasil-enfrenta-rival-chinesa-no-ufc-xangai-em-agosto/|title=Hostile environment! Bruna Brasil faces Chinese rival at UFC Shanghai in August|publisher=agfight.com|author=Gaspar Bruno|date=2025-06-12|access-date=2025-06-12}} {{in lang|pt-br}} However, the pairing was pulled in order to serve as the featured bout of the [[Road to UFC Season 4]] semifinals event a day earlier.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-returns-shanghai-blockbuster-main-event-battle-ranking-light-heavyweights-johnny-walker-12|title=UFC returns to Shanghai with a blockbuster main event battle of
